#+begin_ask :id formal-first-boundary :title "Where should the next formal boundary begin?" :options "A small pure core|Module contracts first|Actor protocols first"
The recommendation is a small pure core and module contracts together. The existing term interface can serve all three paths. This question records a research preference; it does not approve a production semantic change.
#+end_ask

#+begin_roster :id formal-work :source "ATLAS.bl.org" :title "The work, with evidence beside it" :start FL-EQUALITY :who "integration"
#+end_roster

* The model, running
These cells execute the actual shared interface. Their stored results are written by =bl doc run=. They establish the stated prototype examples and the evidence rules below. The native engine answers retain their original unchecked-certificate status; a separate cell exercises the new small certificate checker.

#+begin_src beam-lisp :id story-common-question
(ns formal.dossier-story
 (:require [formal.term :as t] [formal.query :as q] [formal.logic :as logic] [formal.backend :as backend]
           [formal.collections :as c] [formal.value :as v] [bl.json :as json] [decimal :as exact-decimal]
           [formal.beam-float :as beam-float] [formal.source :as term-source] [formal.source-runtime :as source-run]
           [formal.point :as point] [formal.data-graph :as data-graph] [formal.runtime-values :as runtime-values]
           [formal.question-graph :as question-graph] [formal.request-record :as request-record]
           [veritas.property :as property] [veritas :as veritas] [veritas.ledger :as verdict-ledger] [veritas.mock :as response-mock] [veritas.fault :as response-fault] [datom] [formal.check :as checked-query]
           [formal.temporal :as temporal] [datom.time :as time] [formal.certificate :as certificate]
           [formal.affine :as affine] [formal.preparation :as preparation] [formal.evaluate :as term-replay] [system.linear :as linear]
           [formal.api :as formal] [formal.core :as core] [formal.identity :as data-identity]
           [formal.core-symbolic :as core-symbolic] [formal.elaborate :as elaborate] [compiler :as compiler] [system.ir :as ir]
           [formal.core-contract-examples :as composed] [formal.core-translation-examples :as rewrites]
           [formal.loop-core :as loop-core] [formal.core-actor-examples :as actors]
           [formal.core-observation-examples :as observed-actors]
           [formal.core-counterexample-examples :as native-traces]
           [system.core :as system] [system.formal :as system-formal] [oracle]
           [formal.answer-examples :as answer-examples]
           [formal.graph-consumer-examples :as graph-examples]
           [formal.term-forest-examples :as forest-examples]
           [formal.term-forest-evaluation-examples :as forest-point-examples]
           [formal.graph-observation-examples :as graph-observation-examples]
           [formal.bounded-point-examples :as bounded-point-examples]
           [formal.numeric-point-examples :as numeric-point-examples]
           [dossier.notebook :as notebook]
           [formal.native-context-examples :as owner-examples]
           [formal.effect-examples :as effect-examples]))
(def x (t/variable "dossier.x" t/Int))
(def question (q/prove (t/gt (t/add x (t/int 1)) x)
               {:id :dossier/increment :profile :math/unbounded-int-v1}))
(def paired (mapv (fn [engine] (logic/run {:backend engine} question)) [:z3 :oxiz]))
(assert (every? (fn [answer] (= :proved (:verdict answer))) paired))
{:question "Adding one increases an unbounded integer."
 :z3 (:verdict (first paired)) :oxiz (:verdict (second paired))}
#+end_src
#+RESULTS: story-common-question
: {:question "Adding one increases an unbounded integer.", :z3 :proved, :oxiz :proved}


#+begin_src beam-lisp :id story-failure-and-room
(def xs (c/vector-symbolic "dossier.full" 2 t/Int))
(def appended (c/vector-conj xs (t/int 7)))
(def full (t/eq (v/term (c/vector-length xs)) (t/int 2)))
(def capacity-question
 (q/prove (t/all [(v/defined appended) (t/not (v/within appended))])
  {:id :dossier/room :assumptions (conj (v/constraints xs) full)}))
(def capacity-result (logic/run {:backend :z3} capacity-question))
(assert (= :proved (:verdict capacity-result)))
{:operation "Append a value to a vector."
 :language-success true :outside-this-two-slot-model true
 :evidence (:verdict capacity-result)}
#+end_src
#+RESULTS: story-failure-and-room
: {:operation "Append a value to a vector.", :evidence :proved, :language-success true, :outside-this-two-slot-model true}


#+begin_src beam-lisp :id story-proof-authority
(assert (every? (fn [answer] (= false (:trusted answer))) paired))
(assert (every? (fn [answer] (= :not-checked (get-in answer [:certificate :status]))) paired))
{:solver-agreement true :compiler-proof-authority false
 :certificate-status :not-checked
 :meaning "These native solver answers have no independently checked solver certificate."}
#+end_src
#+RESULTS: story-proof-authority
: {:compiler-proof-authority false, :solver-agreement true, :certificate-status :not-checked, :meaning "These native solver answers have no independently checked solver certificate."}

#+begin_src beam-lisp :id story-checked-certificate
(def proof-question
 (q/prove (t/ge (t/add (t/mul (t/int 2) x) (t/int 3)) (t/int 7))
  {:assumptions [(t/ge x (t/int 2))] :profile :bl/boolean-linear-integer-v1}))
(def checked-proof (formal/certify proof-question))
(assert (= :checked (:status checked-proof)))
(assert (= :checked (:status (formal/check-certificate proof-question (:certificate checked-proof)))))
{:proof (:status checked-proof)
 :meaning "The term claim follows from its assumptions in the checked fragment."
 :feasibility (get-in checked-proof [:checking :feasibility])
 :source-connection (get-in checked-proof [:checking :source-connection])
 :compiler-proof-authority false}
#+end_src
#+RESULTS: story-checked-certificate
: {:proof :checked, :compiler-proof-authority false, :source-connection :not-established, :feasibility :not-established, :meaning "The term claim follows from its assumptions in the checked fragment."}

#+begin_src beam-lisp :id story-specified-core
(def prepared-core
 (elaborate/prepare "(let [next (+ balance 1)] (>= next 1))" {"balance" t/Int}))
(assert (= :elaborated (:status prepared-core)))
(def reference-core
 (core/evaluate (:core prepared-core) {"balance" t/Int} {"balance" 4}))
(def symbolic-core (core-symbolic/lower (:core prepared-core) {"balance" t/Int}))
(def core-question
 (q/prove (core-symbolic/as-value symbolic-core)
  {:profile core/profile
   :assumptions [(t/ge (get-in symbolic-core [:inputs "balance"]) (t/int 0))]}))
(def core-proof (formal/certify core-question))
(assert (= :checked (:status core-proof)))
{:core (get-in prepared-core [:typing :status])
 :reference-result (:status reference-core) :value (:value reference-core)
 :term-proof (:status core-proof)
 :source-assurance :reviewed-not-mechanically-proved
 :compiler-proof-authority false}
#+end_src
#+RESULTS: story-specified-core
: {:value true, :core :typed, :compiler-proof-authority false, :reference-result :returned, :term-proof :checked, :source-assurance :reviewed-not-mechanically-proved}




#+begin_src beam-lisp :id story-program-contract
(def withdrawal-contract
 (formal/program-contract
  {:name "dossier-withdrawal"
   :parameters {"balance" t/Int "amount" t/Int}
   :source "(- balance amount)"
   :requires (fn [in]
               (t/all [(t/ge (get in "balance") (t/int 0))
                       (t/ge (get in "amount") (t/int 0))
                       (t/le (get in "amount") (get in "balance"))]))
   :ensures (fn [in result] (t/ge result (t/int 0)))
   :returns :must :effects #{}}))
(def withdrawal-proof (formal/verify-contract withdrawal-contract))
(assert (= :checked (:status withdrawal-proof)))
{:contract (:status withdrawal-proof)
 :meaning "The withdrawal result is nonnegative under its stated precondition."
 :assumption-consistency (:assumption-consistency withdrawal-proof)
 :source-connection (:source-connection withdrawal-proof)
 :compiler-proof-authority (:compiler-proof-authority withdrawal-proof)}
#+end_src
#+RESULTS: story-program-contract
: {:compiler-proof-authority false, :assumption-consistency :not-checked, :source-connection :reviewed-not-mechanically-proved, :contract :checked, :meaning "The withdrawal result is nonnegative under its stated precondition."}


#+begin_src beam-lisp :id story-loop-argument
(def countdown-contract
 (formal/loop-contract
  {:name "dossier-countdown" :parameters {"n" t/Int}
   :source "(loop [i n acc 0] (if (> i 0) (recur (- i 1) (+ acc 1)) acc))"
   :requires (fn [in] (t/ge (get in "n") (t/int 0)))
   :invariant (fn [in state]
                (t/all [(t/ge (get state "i") (t/int 0))
                        (t/eq (t/add (get state "i") (get state "acc")) (get in "n"))]))
   :rank (fn [in state] [(get state "i")])
   :ensures (fn [in state result] (t/eq result (get in "n")))}))
(def countdown-argument (formal/verify-loop countdown-contract))
(assert (= :checked (:status countdown-argument)))
(assert (= :checked-obligations
           (:status (formal/check-loop-proof countdown-contract (:proof countdown-argument)))))
{:loop-model (:status countdown-argument)
 :scope (:scope countdown-argument)
 :ranking-dimension (get-in countdown-argument [:checking :ranking-rule :dimension])

#+begin_src beam-lisp :id story-protocol-invariant
(def counter-protocol
 (formal/protocol
  {:name "dossier-counter" :state {"count" t/Int}
   :init (fn [s] (t/eq (get s "count") (t/int 0)))
   :invariant (fn [s] (t/ge (get s "count") (t/int 0)))
   :transitions [{:name "add" :inputs {"amount" t/Int}
                  :assumptions (fn [s in] [(t/ge (get in "amount") (t/int 0))])
                  :next (fn [s in] {"count" (t/add (get s "count") (get in "amount"))})}]}))
(def counter-argument (formal/check {:initial-state {"count" 0}} counter-protocol))
(def counter-trace
 (formal/replay-protocol-trace counter-protocol {"count" 0}
  [{:transition "add" :inputs {"amount" 2}} {:stutter true}]))
(assert (= :checked (:status counter-argument)))
(assert (= :checked-obligations
           (:status (formal/check-protocol-proof counter-protocol (:proof counter-argument)))))
(assert (= :holds (:safety counter-trace)))
{:protocol-model (:status counter-argument)
 :scope (:scope counter-argument)
 :example-trace (:status counter-trace) :example-safety (:safety counter-trace)
 :final-state (:state counter-trace) :liveness (:liveness counter-argument)
 :source-connection (:source-connection counter-argument)
 :compiler-proof-authority false}
#+end_src

: {:scope :all-finite-prefixes-of-the-declared-state-model, :liveness :not-established, :compiler-proof-authority false, :source-connection :authored-model-not-live-beam-actor, :protocol-model :checked, :example-trace :replayed, :example-safety :holds, :final-state {"count" 2}}

 (formal/protocol
  {:name "dossier-later-failure" :state {"i" t/Int}
   :init (fn [s] (t/eq (get s "i") (t/int 0)))
   :invariant (fn [s] (t/lt (get s "i") (t/int 2)))

#+begin_src beam-lisp :id story-binding-context
(def context-source
 "(fn [s msg] (assoc s \"n\" (+ (get s \"n\") (get msg \"amount\")) \"flag\" (not (get s \"flag\"))))")
(def context-declaration
 (formal/source-context context-source
  [["state" (core/record-sort {"n" t/Int "flag" t/Bool})]
   ["message" (core/record-sort {"amount" t/Int})]]))
(def context-function
 (formal/program-contract
  {:name "dossier-context-function" :context context-declaration
   :ensures (fn [input output]
              (t/eq (get output "n") (t/add (get-in input ["state" "n"]) (get-in input ["message" "amount"]))))}))
(def context-spec
 {:name "dossier-context-handler" :state {"n" t/Int "flag" t/Bool}
  :handlers [{:name "step" :inputs {"amount" t/Int} :source context-source}]})
(def context-ordinary (formal/source-protocol context-spec))
(def context-linked (formal/source-protocol (assoc context-spec :bindings :closed-records)))
(def context-comparison
 (let [child (BeamLisp.Env/fork)]
  (try
   (BeamLisp.Env/with_env child
    (fn []
     (BeamLisp.Env/intern "formal.core.intrinsics" "assoc" (fn [& arguments] {"n" 99 "flag" true}))
     (mapv #(formal/observe-protocol-trace % {"n" 0 "flag" true}
              [{:transition "step" :inputs {"amount" 2}}]) [context-ordinary context-linked])))
   (finally (BeamLisp.Env/destroy child)))))
(assert (= :checked-context (:status (formal/check-source-context context-declaration))))
(assert (= :checked (:status (formal/check context-function))))
(assert (= [:disagreement :observed] (mapv :status context-comparison)))
(assert (= (:identity context-declaration) (get-in context-linked [:handlers 0 :context :identity])))
(assert (every? #(= false (get-in % [:receipt :cleanup :actor-alive])) context-comparison))
(assert (every? #(every? (fn [m] (and (:retired m) (not (:loaded m))))
                         (get-in % [:receipt :cleanup :modules])) context-comparison))
{:binding-context :checked-context :function-contract :checked
 :ordinary-when-rebound (:status (first context-comparison))
 :linked-when-rebound (:status (second context-comparison))
 :linked-state (get-in (second context-comparison) [:receipt :frames 1 :outcome :value])
 :body-closure (get-in (second context-comparison) [:checking :binding-context :closure])
 :transitive-runtime-closure :not-established :ambient-equivalence :requires-declared-bindings
 :compiler-proof-authority false}
#+end_src

#+begin_src beam-lisp :id story-specialization
(def capture-context
 (formal/selected-source-context
  {:sources [{:namespace 'formal.dossier.capture :revision 'current :file "capture.bl"
              :source "(ns formal.dossier.capture) (def bias 40) (defn- helper [x] (erlang/+ bias x)) (defn answer [x] (let [bias 100] (helper x)))"}]
   :entry ['formal.dossier.capture 'current] :target 'answer :parameters [["input" t/Int]]}))
(def captured-program (:prepared (formal/prepare-source-context capture-context)))
(def reduction (get-in captured-program [:specialization :object]))
(def reduction-check (formal/check-specialization reduction))
(def captured-name (get-in reduction [:anf :name]))
(def caller-name
 (:name (first (filter #(and (= :let (:op %)) (= :lit (get-in % [:init :op])) (= 100 (get-in % [:init :val])))
                 (tree-seq ir/node? ir/child-nodes (:anf reduction))))))
(assert (string? caller-name))
(def changed-anf
 (ir/rewrite (fn [node] (if (and (= :var (:op node)) (= captured-name (:name node))) (assoc node :name caller-name) node)) (:anf reduction)))
(def changed-reduction (data-identity/seal (assoc reduction :anf changed-anf)))
(def changed-reduction-check (formal/check-specialization changed-reduction))
(def captured-inputs (into {} (map (fn [[alias role]] [alias (core/variable role t/Int)]) (:input-aliases reduction-check))))
(def changed-core (core/canonicalize (elaborate/from-anf changed-anf captured-inputs) {"input" t/Int}))
(def changed-late-pair (data-identity/seal (assoc (get-in captured-program [:elaboration :object]) :anf changed-anf :core changed-core)))
(def changed-late-check (formal/check-elaboration changed-late-pair))
(def changed-value (:value (core/evaluate changed-core {"input" t/Int} {"input" 2})))
(def capture-promise
 (formal/program-contract {:name "dossier-helper-capture" :context capture-context
  :ensures (fn [in out] (t/eq out (t/add (get in "input") (t/int 40))))}))
(def capture-proof (formal/check capture-promise))
(def capture-call (formal/observe-contract capture-promise {"input" 2}))
(assert (= :checked (:status reduction-check)))
(assert (= :rejected (:status changed-reduction-check)))
(assert (= :checked (:status changed-late-check)))
(assert (= 102 changed-value))
(assert (= :checked (:status capture-proof)))
(assert (= :observed (:status capture-call)))
(assert (= 42 (get-in capture-call [:receipt :outcome :value])))
{:unit-anf (:status reduction-check) :promise (:status capture-proof)
 :original-source-value (get-in capture-call [:receipt :outcome :value])
 :changed-capture-value changed-value :changed-unit-anf (:status changed-reduction-check)
 :changed-anf-core (:status changed-late-check)
 :unit-wellformedness (:unit-wellformedness reduction-check)
 :checker-soundness (:soundness reduction-check) :compiler-proof-authority false}

#+begin_src beam-lisp :id story-sdk-interface
(def sdk-portable (system/prove-box {:initial-state {"count" 0}} counter-protocol))
(def sdk-native (mapv #(system/preserves? {:backend %} counter-protocol "add") [:z3 :oxiz]))
(def sdk-question (system-formal/preservation-question counter-protocol "add"))
(def sdk-seen (atom []))
(def sdk-handled
 (oracle/with-query-handler
  (fn [question execute]
   (swap! sdk-seen conj question)
   ((oracle/query-table {}) question execute))
  (fn [] (system/preserves? {:backend :oxiz :worker "/unavailable/formal-worker"}
                            counter-protocol "add"))))
(assert (= :checked (:status sdk-portable)))

(assert (= [sdk-question] @sdk-seen))
(assert (= :unknown (:verdict sdk-handled)))

 :native-verdicts (mapv :verdict sdk-native) :same-question (= [sdk-question] @sdk-seen)
 :table-answer (:verdict sdk-handled) :handler-native-worker :not-opened
 :handler-scope :calling-process :handler-certificate (get-in sdk-handled [:certificate :status])

(def packaged-portable-record (json/decode (File/read! (Path/join packaged-evidence-root "portable-runtime-artifact.json"))))

            (get-in packaged-portable-record [component "producer_sha256"])))

#+begin_src beam-lisp :id story-session-lifetime
(def ended-native-sessions
 (mapv (fn [engine]
        (let [escaped (atom nil)
              original-error (try
                              (backend/with-session {:backend engine}
                               (fn [session]
                                (reset! escaped session)
                                (throw (ex-info "the program's own error" {:case :dossier-callback}))))
                              (catch error (= {:case :dossier-callback} (ex-data error))))
              worker-path (str "/proc/" (:pid (:hello @escaped)))
              deadline (+ (erlang/monotonic_time :millisecond) 3000)]
         (loop []
          (when (and (File/exists? worker-path) (< (erlang/monotonic_time :millisecond) deadline))
           (Process/sleep 10) (recur)))
         (assert original-error)
         (assert (not (Process/alive? (:pid @escaped))))
         (assert (not (File/exists? worker-path)))
         {:callback-error (if original-error :preserved :lost)
          :owner-alive (Process/alive? (:pid @escaped)) :worker-alive (File/exists? worker-path)
          :retired (:retired @(:state @escaped))})) [:z3 :oxiz]))
(into-sorted-map
 {:backends [:z3 :oxiz] :callback-errors (mapv :callback-error ended-native-sessions)
  :owner-alive (mapv :owner-alive ended-native-sessions) :worker-alive (mapv :worker-alive ended-native-sessions)
  :retired (mapv :retired ended-native-sessions) :busy-solver-cancellation :not-qualified
  :compiler-proof-authority false})
#+end_src
#+RESULTS: story-session-lifetime
: {:backends [:z3 :oxiz], :busy-solver-cancellation :not-qualified, :callback-errors [:preserved :preserved], :compiler-proof-authority false, :owner-alive [false false], :retired [true true], :worker-alive [false false]}



#+begin_src beam-lisp :id story-source-properties
(def source-floor '(defn floor [c] (if (< c 0) 0 c)))
(def source-dom (veritas/one-of -2))
(def source-laws (mapv #(property/prove {:backend %} source-floor '(>= result 0) source-dom) [:z3 :oxiz]))
(def source-failures (mapv #(property/prove {:backend %} source-floor '(> result 0) source-dom) [:z3 :oxiz]))

(assert (every? #(= :refuted (:modality %)) source-failures))
(assert (apply = (map :question-identity source-laws)))
(assert (apply = (map :question-identity source-failures)))
(into-sorted-map
 {:backends [:z3 :oxiz] :law (mapv :modality source-laws)
  :native-certificate (mapv #(get-in % [:answer :certificate :status]) source-laws)
  :same-complete-question true :failing-inputs (mapv :witness source-failures)
  :actual-results (mapv :result source-failures)
  :term-replay (mapv #(get-in % [:answer :validation :status]) source-failures)
  :runtime-replay (mapv #(get-in % [:runtime-replay :status]) source-failures)
  :source-connection :reviewed :compiler-proof-authority false})

#+begin_src beam-lisp :id story-source-property-outcomes
(def source-crashes
 (mapv #(property/prove {:backend %} '(defn boom [n] (quot n 0)) true (veritas/one-of 7)) [:z3 :oxiz]))
(def source-empty
 (mapv #(property/prove {:backend %} '(defn ident [n] n) false (veritas/one-of)) [:z3 :oxiz]))
(def source-refusal (property/prove {} '(defn ident [n] n) '(string? (str result)) source-dom))
(def source-sampled (property/prove {:fallback :sample} '(defn ident [n] n) '(string? (str result)) source-dom))
(def source-unknown
 (oracle/with-query-handler
  (fn [question _] (oracle/query-answer question {:phase :query :solver-status :unknown :verdict :unknown}))
  #(property/prove {:worker "/unavailable/formal-worker" :fallback :sample} source-floor '(>= result 0) source-dom)))
(assert (every? #(= :refuted (:modality %)) source-crashes))
(into-sorted-map
 {:target-crash (mapv #(get-in % [:runtime-replay :target :status]) source-crashes)
  :crash-replay (mapv #(get-in % [:runtime-replay :status]) source-crashes)
  :empty-domain (mapv :modality source-empty) :unsupported (:modality source-refusal)
  :explicit-sample (:modality source-sampled) :sample-engine (:engine source-sampled)
  :unknown (:modality source-unknown) :unknown-engine (:engine source-unknown)
  :compiler-proof-authority false})

#+begin_src beam-lisp :id story-clause-coverage
(def covered-clauses "(defn sign ([x] :when (>= x 0) (unavailable-body)) ([renamed] :when (< renamed 0) (another-unavailable-body)))")
(def gap-clauses "(defn sign ([x] :when (> x 0) :positive) ([renamed] :when (< renamed 0) :negative))")

(def gap-answers (mapv #(veritas/covers {:backend % :engine :terms} gap-clauses (veritas/int-of -100 100)) [:z3 :oxiz]))

(assert (every? #(and (= :refuted (:modality %)) (= 0 (:witness %))) gap-answers))
(into-sorted-map
 {:coverage (mapv :modality coverage-answers) :gap (mapv :modality gap-answers)
  :uncovered-inputs (mapv :witness gap-answers) :clause-local-names :preserved
  :original-bodies :removed-before-compilation :compiler-proof-authority false})

#+begin_src beam-lisp :id story-recorded-verdicts
(def found-nil (veritas/exists {:engine :enumerate} "x" (veritas/one-of nil false) '(nil? x)))
(def found-false (veritas/exists {:engine :enumerate} "x" (veritas/one-of false nil) '(= x false)))
(def sampled-absence (veritas/exists {:engine :sample :seed 7 :n 10} "x" (veritas/any-of) false))
(assert (= :witnessed (:modality found-nil)))
(assert (contains? found-nil :witness)) (assert (= nil (:witness found-nil)))
(assert (= false (:witness found-false)))
(assert (= :unknown (:modality sampled-absence)))
(def recorded-summary
 (let [ledger (verdict-ledger/open nil)]
  (try
   (verdict-ledger/record! ledger found-nil {:claim "nil is an input"})
   (verdict-ledger/record! ledger (first composed-answers) {:claim "composed domain law"})
   (let [rows (verdict-ledger/all ledger)]
    (assert (every? #(= :recorded (:verdict/assurance %)) rows))
    {:rows (count rows) :assurance :recorded
     :nil-presence (some #(and (= true (:verdict/witness-present %)) (= nil (:verdict/witness %))) rows)
     :question-retained (some #(= (:question-identity (first composed-answers)) (:verdict/question-id %)) rows)})
   (finally (datom/close! ledger)))))
(into-sorted-map
 {:nil-present (contains? found-nil :witness) :false-witness (:witness found-false)
  :sampled-absence (:modality sampled-absence) :recorded recorded-summary :compiler-proof-authority false})
#+end_src
#+RESULTS: story-recorded-verdicts
: {:compiler-proof-authority false, :false-witness false, :nil-present true, :recorded {:rows 2, :assurance :recorded, :nil-presence true, :question-retained true}, :sampled-absence :unknown}

 {:responses (mapv :modality response-values) :same-complete-question true
  :source-replay (mapv #(get-in % [:runtime-replay :status]) response-values)

#+begin_src beam-lisp :id story-response-ledger
(def response-recorded
 (let [ledger (verdict-ledger/open nil) result (first response-minima)]
  (try
   (verdict-ledger/record! ledger result {:claim "global integer minimum"})
   (let [row (first (verdict-ledger/all ledger)) evidence (:verdict/evidence row)]
    (assert (= false (:verdict/witness-present row)))
    (assert (= :witnessed (get-in evidence [:evidence :feasibility :modality])))
    (assert (= :unsat (get-in evidence [:evidence :terminal :answer :verdict])))
    {:assurance (:verdict/assurance row) :top-level-witness (:verdict/witness-present row)
     :feasible-value (get-in evidence [:evidence :feasibility :value])
     :absence-claim (get-in evidence [:evidence :terminal :answer :verdict])})
   (finally (datom/close! ledger)))))
(into-sorted-map {:compound-record response-recorded :compiler-proof-authority false})

#+begin_src beam-lisp :id story-answer-binding
(def reply-contract (response-mock/contract-from [[:x "Int"]] ['(>= x 0)]))
(def reply-x (v/term (response-mock/field reply-contract :x)))
(def reply-request (response-mock/prepare reply-contract {:assumptions [(t/gt reply-x (t/int 10))]}))
(def reply-other (response-mock/prepare reply-contract {:pins {:x 0}}))
(def reply-results
 (mapv (fn [backend]
         (let [options {:backend backend} answer (logic/run options (:question reply-request))
               good (response-mock/accept-answer reply-request answer)
               foreign (response-mock/accept-answer reply-request (logic/run options (:question reply-other)))
               absent (response-mock/accept-answer reply-request (logic/run options (q/sat {:assumptions [(t/bool false)]})))
               mismatch (response-mock/accept-answer reply-request (assoc-in answer [:witness "field/x" :value] 0))]
          (assert (= (:question reply-request) (:question answer)))
          (assert (= :witnessed (:modality good)))
          (assert (= :valid (get-in good [:projection-replay :status])))
          (assert (= :error (:modality foreign)))
          (assert (= :error (:modality absent)))
          (assert (not= true (:none absent)))
          (assert (= :candidate (:modality mismatch)))

      actual (source-run/run (:anf prepared) [1.0])
      legacy (system/verify-process nil (first (BeamLisp.Reader/read_string
       "(defserver ^{:invariant (= (- (+ x 0.1) x) 0.1)} rounded (init [] (ok 1.0)) (handle-call [:noop] [_ x] (reply :ok x)))")))
      numeric (beam-float/equal :numeric (beam-float/literal 0.0) (beam-float/literal -0.0))
      strict (beam-float/equal :identity (beam-float/literal 0.0) (beam-float/literal -0.0))]
 {:source-profile (:profile prepared) :term-value (:value term) :actual-value (:value actual)
  :module-retired (= false (:module-loaded actual))
  :legacy-source-status (:status legacy) :legacy-source-holds (:holds legacy)
  :zeros-numerically-equal (term-replay/value (v/term numeric) {})
  :zeros-strictly-identical (term-replay/value (v/term strict) {})
  :implementation-correspondence :reviewed-with-concrete-checks :compiler-proof-authority false})

#+begin_src beam-lisp :id story-formal-request
(let [a (t/variable "dossier.request.a" t/Real)
      b (t/variable "dossier.request.b" t/Real)
      subject (question-graph/prepare
               (q/sat {:assumptions [(t/eq a (t/real 1 3)) (t/eq b (t/real 2 5))]
                       :observe {"product" (t/mul a b)}}))
      request (logic/prepare-request subject
                {:adapter {:backend :z3 :solver-ms 500 :hard-ms 2000}})
      compared (logic/compare-request request)
      supplied (logic/with-request-handler
                 (fn [asked fallback]
                  (logic/answer-request asked {"dossier.request.a" (term-replay/real 1 3)
                                               "dossier.request.b" (term-replay/real 2 5)}))
                 #(logic/execute-request request))
      changed (logic/with-request-handler
                (fn [asked fallback]
                 (logic/answer-request asked {"dossier.request.a" (term-replay/real 1 2)
                                              "dossier.request.b" (term-replay/real 2 5)}))
                #(logic/execute-request request))
      reopened (request-record/recheck (request-record/pack request (get-in compared [:z3 :response])))
      rows (mapv
             (fn [engine]
              (let [result (get compared engine)
                    product (get-in result [:point-check :observations 0 :roots 0 :value])]
               (assert (= :sat (:verdict result)))
               (assert (= :valid (get-in result [:point-check :validation :status])))
               (assert (= (term-replay/real 2 15) product))
               (assert (= :matching-response (get-in result [:local-delegation :status])))
               (into-sorted-map
                {:backend engine :verdict (:verdict result) :validation (get-in result [:point-check :validation :status])
                 :product product :same-subject (= (:subject-reference result) (:subject-reference compared))
                 :observations :graph-source-cursors :materialization (get-in result [:point-check :observation-materialization])
                 :delegation (get-in result [:local-delegation :status])
                 :checked-native-execution (:checked-native-execution result)
                 :certificate (get-in result [:certificate :status])}))) [:z3 :oxiz])]
 (assert (= :sat (:verdict supplied) (:verdict reopened)))
 (assert (= 0 (get-in supplied [:local-delegation :attempts])))
 (assert (= :invalid-point (:verdict changed)))
 (into-sorted-map {:subject :one-portable-request :backends rows
                   :supplied-point (:verdict supplied) :supplied-native-attempts (get-in supplied [:local-delegation :attempts])
                   :reopened-point (:verdict reopened) :changed-point (:verdict changed)
                   :compiler-proof-authority false}))
#+end_src

#+begin_src beam-lisp :id story-word-request
(let [word [:bv 8]
      x (t/variable "dossier.word.counter" word)
      zero (t/literal word 0) one (t/literal word 1)
      wrap (t/app :bv-add word [x one] nil)
      caller (question-graph/prepare (q/sat {:assumptions [(t/eq wrap zero)]}))
      policy {:profile logic/numeric-word-policy}
      receipt (json/decode (File/read! "research/formal-logic/evidence/word-integration/records-qualification.json"))
      rows
      (mapv (fn [engine]
              (backend/with-session {:backend engine}
               (fn [session]
                (assert (:ok (backend/load! session [(q/assertion "last-byte" (t/eq x (t/literal word 255)))])))
                (let [saved (atom nil)
                      result (logic/with-context-request session caller {:policy policy}
                               (fn [r execute] (reset! saved r) (execute)))
                      packet (request-record/pack-graph @saved (:response result))
                      replayed (request-record/recheck-graph packet)
                      changed (logic/recheck-request @saved
                                (logic/answer-request @saved {"dossier.word.counter" {:formal/bv [8 0]}}))
                      supplied (logic/with-context-request session caller {:policy policy}
                                 (fn [_ execute]
                                  (logic/with-request-handler
                                   (fn [r _] (logic/answer-request r {"dossier.word.counter" {:formal/bv [8 255]}})) execute)))]
                 (assert (= :sat (:verdict result) (:verdict replayed) (:verdict supplied)))
                 (assert (= :invalid-point (:verdict changed)))
                 (assert (= 0 (get-in supplied [:local-delegation :attempts])))
                 (into-sorted-map {:backend engine :eight-bit-wrap :sat
                   :changed-counter (:verdict changed) :graph-packet-replay (:verdict replayed)
                   :checking-policy (get-in @saved [:policy :profile])
                   :supplied-fallback-attempts (get-in supplied [:local-delegation :attempts])
                   :checked-native-execution (:checked-native-execution replayed) :compiler-proof-authority false})))))
            [:z3 :oxiz])]
 (assert (= 2 (get receipt "fresh_sdk_only_readers")))
 (assert (= 0 (get receipt "solver_exchanges")))
 (assert (= 1 (get receipt "normalizer_calls_per_selected_lookup")))
 (into-sorted-map {:example :word-question :backends rows :word-width 8 :last-counter 255 :next-counter 0
                  :fresh-sdk-readers (get receipt "fresh_sdk_only_readers")
                  :saved-word-records (get receipt "records") :reader-solver-exchanges (get receipt "solver_exchanges")
                  :normalizer-calls-per-selected-lookup (get receipt "normalizer_calls_per_selected_lookup")
                  :compiler-proof-authority false}))
#+end_src

#+begin_src beam-lisp :id story-retained-request
(let [x (t/variable "dossier.retained.x" t/Int)
      caller (question-graph/prepare (q/sat {:assumptions [(t/le x (t/int 9))]}))
      reader-receipt (json/decode (File/read! "research/formal-logic/evidence/retained-request/records-qualification.json"))
      rows
      (mapv (fn [engine]
              (backend/with-session {:backend engine}
               (fn [session]
                (assert (:ok (backend/reset! session)))
                (assert (:ok (backend/load! session [(q/assertion "seven" (t/eq x (t/int 7)))])))
                (let [frozen (atom nil) unused (atom nil)
                      borrowed (logic/with-context-request session caller
                                 (fn [r execute] (reset! frozen r) (execute)))
                      _ (logic/with-context-request session caller (fn [_ execute] (reset! unused execute)))
                      expired (try (@unused) :accepted (catch e (:kind (ex-data e))))
                      changed (logic/recheck-request @frozen (logic/answer-request @frozen {"dossier.retained.x" 8}))
                      caller-request (logic/prepare-request caller {:adapter {:backend engine}})
                      caller-only (logic/recheck-request caller-request (logic/answer-request caller-request {"dossier.retained.x" 8}))
                      reopened (request-record/recheck (request-record/pack @frozen (:response borrowed)))
                      supplied (logic/with-context-request session caller
                                 (fn [_ execute]
                                  (logic/with-request-handler
                                   (fn [r _] (logic/answer-request r {"dossier.retained.x" 7})) execute)))]
                 (assert (= :sat (:verdict borrowed) (:verdict reopened) (:verdict supplied) (:verdict caller-only)))
                 (assert (= :invalid-point (:verdict changed)))
                 (assert (= :retained-request-lease expired))
                 (assert (= 3 (get-in borrowed [:response :formal/request-answer])))
                 (assert (= :matching-response (get-in borrowed [:local-delegation :status])))
                 (assert (= 0 (get-in supplied [:local-delegation :attempts])))
                 (into-sorted-map
                  {:backend engine :complete-point (:verdict borrowed) :caller-only-eight (:verdict caller-only)
                   :complete-eight (:verdict changed) :reopened-point (:verdict reopened)
                   :answer-version (get-in borrowed [:response :formal/request-answer])
                   :unused-callback :expired :supplied-fallback-attempts (get-in supplied [:local-delegation :attempts])
                   :checked-native-execution (:checked-native-execution reopened)
                   :compiler-proof-authority false}))))) [:z3 :oxiz])]
 (assert (= 2 (get reader-receipt "fresh_sdk_only_readers")))
 (assert (= 0 (get reader-receipt "solver_exchanges")))
 (into-sorted-map {:subject :retained-complete-request :backends rows
                   :saved-records (get reader-receipt "records")
                   :fresh-sdk-readers (get reader-receipt "fresh_sdk_only_readers")
                   :reader-solver-exchanges (get reader-receipt "solver_exchanges")
                   :compiler-proof-authority false}))
#+end_src

#+begin_src beam-lisp :id story-exact-numbers
(let [triple [(= 1.5M 1.5) (= 1.5 1.50M) (= 1.5M 1.50M)]
      zero-outcome (try (mod 7 0) :returned (catch _ :raised))
      before (json/decode (File/read! "research/formal-logic/evidence/numeric-semantics/baseline/observation-summary.json"))
      after (json/decode (File/read! "research/formal-logic/evidence/numeric-semantics/candidate/observation-summary.json"))
      model (notebook/read-model "research/formal-logic/ATLAS.bl.org")
      values (notebook/calculate model {} "value")]
 (assert (= [true true true] triple))
 (assert (not (exact-decimal/same-representation? 1.5M 1.50M)))
 (assert (= ["1.5M" "1.50M"] [(pr-str 1.5M) (pr-str 1.50M)]))
 (assert (not (= 0.1M 0.1)))
 (assert (= 0.50M 0.5))
 (assert (= 2 (count {1.5M :one 1.50M :two})))
 (assert (= -2 (mod 7 -3)))
 (assert (= :raised zero-outcome))
 (assert (= {"beam" 6 "js-direct" 14 "js-compiled" 6} (get before "differences")))
 (assert (= {"beam" 0 "js-direct" 0 "js-compiled" 0} (get after "differences")))
 (assert (= (get-in before ["differences" "js-direct"]) (get values "numeric_js_before")))
 (assert (= (get-in after ["differences" "js-direct"]) (get values "numeric_js_after")))
 (assert (= 14 (get values "numeric_js_fixed")))
 (into-sorted-map {:subject :exact-numeric-values :transitive-triple triple
                  :same-representation false :retained-scale [(pr-str 1.5M) (pr-str 1.50M)]
                  :exact-tenth (= 0.1M 0.1) :exact-half (= 0.50M 0.5)
                  :raw-map-key-count (count {1.5M :one 1.50M :two})
                  :floor-modulus (mod 7 -3) :zero-divisor zero-outcome
                  :measured-js-gaps-before (get values "numeric_js_before")
                  :measured-js-gaps-after (get values "numeric_js_after")
                  :compiler-proof-authority false}))
#+end_src

#+begin_src beam-lisp :id story-message-migration
(def message-migration
 (formal/message-migration
  {:name "dossier-message" :parameters [["message" t/Int]] :message "message"
   :source-function '(fn [x] (+ x 1))
   :admit-before (fn [in] (t/ge (get in "message") (t/int 0)))
   :admit-after (fn [_ output] (t/ge (v/term output) (t/int 0)))}))
(def message-bad
 (formal/message-migration
  {:name "dossier-message-bad" :parameters [["message" t/Int]] :message "message"
   :source-function '(fn [x] (- x 1000))
   :admit-before (fn [in] (t/ge (get in "message") (t/int 0)))
   :admit-after (fn [_ output] (t/ge (v/term output) (t/int 0)))}))
(def message-good-results (mapv #(formal/check {:backend % :admission {"message" 0}} message-migration) [:z3 :oxiz]))
(def message-bad-results (mapv #(formal/check {:backend %} message-bad) [:z3 :oxiz]))
(def message-limited (mapv #(formal/check {:backend % :search {:nodes 1} :admission {"message" 0}} message-migration) [:z3 :oxiz]))
(def message-actual (formal/replay-migration-source message-migration {"message" 4}))
(assert (= [true true] (mapv :verified message-good-results)))
(assert (= [false false] (mapv :verified message-bad-results)))
(assert (= [nil nil] (mapv :verified message-limited)))
(assert (= :valid (:status message-actual)))
(assert (= 5 (get-in message-actual [:actual :value])))
(into-sorted-map
 {:backends [:z3 :oxiz] :positive (mapv :status message-good-results) :negative (mapv :status message-bad-results)
  :verified-positive (mapv :verified message-good-results) :verified-negative (mapv :verified message-bad-results)
  :native-only (mapv :status message-limited) :verified-native-only (mapv :verified message-limited)
  :fresh-certificates (mapv #(get-in % [:proof :checking :status]) message-good-results)
  :admission (mapv #(get-in % [:admission :checking :status]) message-good-results)
  :source-replay (:status message-actual) :actual-value (get-in message-actual [:actual :value])
  :module-retired (= false (get-in message-actual [:actual :module-loaded]))
  :delivery-authority false :compiler-proof-authority false})
#+end_src
#+RESULTS: story-message-migration

#+begin_src beam-lisp :id story-temporal-values
(def appointment (temporal/shift (temporal/span 0 1) (temporal/endpoint-range "appointment.start" 0 4)))
(def appointment-window (temporal/span 2 3))
(def appointment-answers (mapv #(time/overlap-result {:backend %} appointment appointment-window) [:z3 :oxiz]))
(def appointment-yes (mapv #(get-in % [:answers :intersection]) appointment-answers))
(def appointment-no (mapv #(get-in % [:answers :separation]) appointment-answers))
(assert (every? #(= :possible (:verdict %)) appointment-answers))
(assert (every? #(= :valid (get-in % [:validation :status])) (concat appointment-yes appointment-no)))
(assert (every? #(time/disjoint? (get-in % [:witness "left" :value]) (get-in % [:witness "right" :value])) appointment-no))
(assert (apply = (mapv #(data-identity/hash (:questions %)) appointment-answers)))

  :intersecting-starts (mapv #(get-in % [:witness "left" :value :from]) appointment-yes)

#+begin_src beam-lisp :id story-temporal-chronology
(def event-bounds {:left/event [0 0] :right/event [1 1] 'left/event [2 2]})
(def event-order [[:left/event :right/event] [:right/event 'left/event]])
(def event-answers (mapv #(time/consistency-result {:backend %} event-bounds event-order) [:z3 :oxiz]))
(assert (every? #(= {:left/event 0 :right/event 1 'left/event 2} (:schedule %)) event-answers))
(def unresolved-time
 (oracle/with-query-handler
  (fn [question _] (oracle/query-answer question {:solver-status :unknown}))
  (fn []
   (let [options {:backend :oxiz :worker "/unavailable/temporal-worker"}
         answer (time/consistency-result options {:event [0 3]} [])
         refused (try (time/consistent? options {:event [0 3]} []) false
                    (catch error (= :temporal-inconclusive (:kind (ex-data error)))))]
    (assert refused)
    {:verdict (:verdict answer) :consistent (:consistent answer) :predicate-refused refused}))))
(into-sorted-map
 {:native-consistency (mapv :consistent event-answers)
  :full-event-keys (mapv pr-str [:left/event :right/event 'left/event])
  :starts (mapv #(get (:schedule %) 'left/event) event-answers)
  :unresolved unresolved-time :compiler-proof-authority false})
#+end_src

#+begin_src beam-lisp :id story-temporal-laws
(def temporal-document (first (filter #(String/ends_with? % ".bl.org") (BeamLisp/argv))))
(def temporal-evidence (Path/join (Path/dirname (Path/expand temporal-document)) "evidence/temporal"))
(def temporal-law-bytes (File/read! (Path/join temporal-evidence "laws.etf")))
(def temporal-law-pin (json/decode (File/read! (Path/join temporal-evidence "laws-pin.json"))))
(assert (= (get temporal-law-pin "bytes") (erlang/byte_size temporal-law-bytes)))
(assert (= (get temporal-law-pin "sha256")
           (Base/encode16 (crypto/hash :sha256 temporal-law-bytes) (list (tuple :case :lower)))))
;; Trusted local artifact produced by temporal-check, matched to its byte pin.
(def temporal-law-data (erlang/binary_to_term temporal-law-bytes))
(assert (= :temporal/laws-v1 (:schema temporal-law-data)))
(def temporal-law-checks
 (mapv (fn [row]
         (let [checked (certificate/check (:question row) (:certificate row))
               ready (formal/term-refutation (:question row) (:certificate row))
               packet (:packet ready)
               connected (formal/check-term-refutation-data (:question row) (:certificate row) packet)
               recorded (json/decode (File/read! (Path/join temporal-evidence (str "law-execution/" (:id row) "/result.json"))))]
          (assert (= :checked (:status checked)))
          (assert (= :checked-data-connection (:status connected)))
          (assert (= "checked-term-entailment" (get recorded "status")))
          (assert (= (:identity packet) (get recorded "packet")))
          {:law (:id row) :certificate (:status checked) :connection (:status connected)})) (:rows temporal-law-data)))
(into-sorted-map
 {:laws (mapv :law temporal-law-checks) :certificates (mapv :certificate temporal-law-checks)
  :original-term-connections (mapv :connection temporal-law-checks)
  :external-proof :recorded-Lean-results :model-runtime-connection :reviewed-implementation
  :compiler-proof-authority false})
#+end_src
#+RESULTS: story-temporal-laws
: {:certificates [:checked :checked :checked], :compiler-proof-authority false, :external-proof :recorded-Lean-results, :laws ["intersection-symmetry" "shift-preserves-duration" "empty-spans-same-points"], :model-runtime-connection :reviewed-implementation, :original-term-connections [:checked-data-connection :checked-data-connection :checked-data-connection]}

* The presentation language
The dossier uses the kit's current Forest design language. These specimens come from its token module. They concern readability and interaction; their measurements are independent of the formal-logic results.

#+begin_night :design forest :title "Readable surfaces in daylight and at night"
#+end_night

The source-backed atlas below keeps the detailed interfaces, semantic alternatives and original receipts. The guided chapters above explain their larger purpose.

** The promise
#+begin_lede
Write a contract in Enchant, inspect its terms, ask either solver the same question, and receive a witness or proof result in language values. The portable term graph belongs to Enchant. Native ASTs belong to one owned backend session.

The atlas separates the language we have, the language we may choose, and the machinery that has actually been qualified. A new API can improve semantics while the language is in preview; compatibility is a design input, not a command to preserve an incoherent law.
#+end_lede

#+begin_stats
46 | interface families | semantic theories, procedures and operational observations
9 | semantic design reviews | one runtime-confirmed equality defect
2 | backend adapters | one portable query contract
30 | runtime canaries | concrete SDK observations, separate from theory qualification
#+end_stats

#+begin_honest :title "What the atlas establishes"
These cards separate proposed interfaces, implemented fragments, and retained qualification. The original canary SDK produced 30 concrete semantic observations, including an equality defect; Codebase indexed 32 of 33 requested files. Source and runtime identities remain separate, and a concrete canary does not qualify a general theory. Raw evidence is linked below; it has not been rewritten to match proposals.
#+end_honest

** What native means for Beam Lisp
The common interface gives Beam Lisp ownership of its symbolic vocabulary, language outcomes, sharing, source maps and evidence. Both native workers can consume that vocabulary: Z3 has native C AST constructors and OxiZ has Rust term APIs. Both still need an adapter from BL data and an owned native lifetime. Native ASTs remain private to the worker while the immutable BL question can travel, be inspected, cached and replayed.

This architecture makes finite maps, tagged outcomes, coverage, permission relations, induction and synthesis reusable language features above either engine. Backend differences remain concrete questions about supported constructors, solver strength, observations, cores and retained artifact behavior. An OxiZ source module or a Z3 theory name alone establishes none of those results; the two corpora below provide the comparison at explicit scopes. Performance and memory advantages require their own measured workload evidence.

** What the original runtime canaries taught us
#+begin_honest :title "The original defect and the qualified numeric repair"
The original canary SDK returned [true true false] for [(= 1.5M 1.5) (= 1.5 1.50M) (= 1.5M 1.50M)]. That exposed a scale-sensitive two-Decimal exception inside an otherwise exact-rational numeric family. The selected numeric SDK removes that exception and adds explicit representation equality. The original canary receipt stays unchanged; the separate numeric qualification establishes the new finite-number behavior.

Cross-list/vector value equality is a separate design choice: it can remain useful even when conj and type-sensitive operations distinguish the representations. Compiler rewrites must use a relation that respects the observable context.
#+end_honest

#+begin_rosetta
Observed call | Original canary SDK result | Design consequence
Decimal/Float equality bridge | true, true, false | generic equality needs a coherent numeric policy
Decomposed e+acute | 1 grapheme, 2 codepoints, 3 bytes | indices and lengths need named text units
mod with negative divisor | mod(7,-3)=-2; mod(-7,-3)=-1 | BL modulus cannot alias SMT Euclidean mod
Finite float exceptional paths | overflow and divide-by-zero raised ArithmeticError | Real and raw IEEE are different source-semantic profiles
Signed binary64 zero | distinct ETF bits, generic equality true | numeric and representation equality remain separate
Vector assoc at count | [10] becomes [10 20] | replacement and append deserve reviewed operations
#+end_rosetta

Raw observations: [[file:evidence/semantic-canaries.json][semantic-canaries.json]]. The 30 calls establish their concrete behavior, not all laws of those domains. Historical nil labels are null because the first observer used the nil atom as its kind label; the raw receipt is retained unchanged and the observer now uses text.

Source discovery: [[file:evidence/source-index.json][source-index.json]] used the pinned Codebase API over the source snapshot at c3ec2255c82413120c0cb75055d7b874710e5255. It observed 32 of 33 requested files; priv/boot/core.bl failed with an if-let macro-binding error. system.analyze converged in three rounds for minikanren (30 definitions) and typed (48 definitions), with complete local coverage. External/opaque effects and dynamic dispatch still require review; no application is certified by these summaries.

The additional [[file:evidence/prototype-index.json][prototype-index.json]] discovers all 21 current prototype modules through Codebase, with local semantic analysis of formal.value and formal.numeric. It supplements the historical 32/33 core inventory; source evaluation and application startup remain disabled, and external/dynamic calls retain explicit coverage limits.

The permanent [[file:repro/equality-transitivity.bl][equality-transitivity.bl]] reproducer reports the executing SDK's actual result. It exposes the original SDK defect and reports this example coherent after the numeric repair. The new focused equality matrix supplies the broader finite-domain regression evidence; this single triple does not prove the implementation for every value.

The portable semantic-key/semantic-hash APIs already reduce numeric values to exact rationals. Runtime map/set keying is a separate consumer: hash-hash-key currently strips metadata while preserving representation (priv/boot/rt.bl:1541), coll-get/assoc use that path (2120/2236), and host/set calls hash_key. A coherent portable key does not establish coherent lookup in every runtime collection. The numeric equality repair is now separately qualified. A collection-key redesign still needs its own rules and qualification.

#+begin_rosetta
Review gate | Concrete acceptance evidence | Current state
Choose the equality law | Decide numeric value equality, representation/scale equality and the domains of generic = and numeric == | Finite numeric value relation and explicit Decimal representation API qualified; other relations remain under review
Repair the minimized defect | The concrete Decimal/Float bridge becomes [true true true] on a newly qualified exact SDK | [true true true] checked on the selected numeric SDK; historical defect receipt retained
Check algebraic laws | Reflexivity, symmetry, transitivity and equal-value hash agreement across Int, finite binary64, scaled Decimal, signed zero, 2^53 boundaries and nested values | Focused finite equality matrix passes on BEAM; identical 900-pair observations pass on three routes
Check real collection consumers | Map get/assoc/dissoc, set membership/dedup, sorted lookup/order and retained old entries under equivalent numeric keys | Separate from portable semantic-key laws
Check storage behavior | Datom AVET equality/ranges, uniqueness/upsert/retract and lossless stored Decimal scale roundtrip | Datom scale, codec, join, upsert, CAS, retraction and durable reopen checks pass in the numeric batch
Decide retained-data transitions | Explicit collision/value precedence when formerly distinct keys become equal, with rebuild or validation where needed | Design decision before adopting changed key semantics
Bind the resulting claim | Retain source generation, immutable SDK pin, semantic profile and focused test receipts | Exact immutable SDK, source, process and result receipts retained in the numeric qualification
#+end_rosetta

** What the shared interface has executed
The combined run at source d89ab15f6 passed 795 assertions across seven focused files. The native worker was d3bdc1b02a6e98f573b0ec249af033f6eb35f33f5ef87d124845e08d1fc942aa; each row also retains its SDK and Z3-library identity. All final corpora recorded zero decisive solver disagreements and zero invalid models. A passing harness preserves declared gaps and candidate models rather than calling them proofs.

#+begin_rosetta
Corpus | Z3 expected semantic matches | OxiZ expected semantic matches | Remaining evidence
61 language-feature questions | 61: 39 proved, 22 refuted | 56: 35 proved, 21 refuted | OxiZ 5 unknown
95 native mathematical questions | 88: 75 proved, 13 refuted | 55: 44 proved, 11 refuted | Z3 5 candidates, 2 unsupported; OxiZ 5 candidates, 19 unknown, 16 unsupported
31 advanced value questions | 31: 27 proved, 4 refuted | 17: 14 proved, 3 refuted | OxiZ 12 unknown, 2 unsupported
#+end_rosetta

Receipts: [[file:evidence/features.json][features.json]], [[file:evidence/native.json][native.json]], [[file:evidence/advanced.json][advanced.json]]. Case IDs in the cards lead to exact scope, question digest, raw status, model availability, independent replay and artifact identity. These observations qualify linked fragments, never all 46 atlas families. Candidate means raw SAT whose complete language/model replay is unavailable; unsupported and unknown remain explicit. Compiler promotion is disabled and independent UNSAT certificates were not checked.

The five OxiZ feature unknowns are integer/quot-rem-decomposition, integer/bl-mod-negative-divisor, integer/euclidean-and-bl-mod-differ, simulation/counter-representation-step, and transition/disabled-body-is-not-evaluated. Native gaps additionally include exact large rationals, Int/Real conversions, generic Seq, parts of FP/rounding, and incomplete quantified/nonlinear solving. Z3's five native candidates retain array/UF/quantifier replay gaps. Both adapters explicitly refuse strings beyond the SMT alphabet and oversized FP formats in this corpus. Unsupported can name an adapter/API/representation limit; it is not a theorem that the underlying solver can never express that feature.

The earlier 92-case native attempt is preserved in [[file:evidence/native-before-normalization.json][native-before-normalization.json]]. It contains the observed OxiZ NaN-payload equality mismatch and invalid replay, plus one extra incomplete Z3 scalar observation. The final artifact canonicalizes SMT NaN literal import and explicitly completes Z3 scalar models. Final success does not erase that failed attempt or promise preservation of raw NaN payloads.

#+begin_rosetta
Capability level | What exists or was observed | What remains a separate claim
Source present | Public term, query, backend, logic, facade and source definitions with current hashes | A method name alone does not establish correct semantics or native support
Typed model constructed | Sort-checked terms, ordinary schema data and explicit value/domain descriptors | Not every runtime BL value or source construct has that model
Source translated | One expression through compiler ANF with admitted Int/Bool/String or nullable bindings | Arbitrary callers, loops, effects, source FP/Decimal and higher-order functions remain outside qualification
Backend executed | Exact per-case problem and native artifact recorded in three corpora | A bounded or finite-universe question is not an unbounded whole-family theorem
Witness replayed | Concrete candidates evaluated independently where the replay vocabulary supports them | Raw SAT, model concreteness and complete replay remain separate
Certificate and authority | Solver answer labels and disabled compiler promotion are explicit | No independent UNSAT certificate or runtime authority was granted
#+end_rosetta

The common facade is formal.api: symbols declares a caller-prefixed schema, question builds a portable obligation from typed values, describe exposes its terms and scope, and check chooses the backend. Pure BL callbacks build the model; executing a callback is not a proof about arbitrary callback implementation code. construction-capabilities exposes the native advertisement, while the retained corpora establish tested capabilities.

This static example documents the implemented low-level interface; it is not an executed document cell or a handwritten solver receipt.

#+begin_src clojure
(ns example.formal
 (:require [formal.term :as t] [formal.query :as q] [formal.logic :as logic]))
(let [x (t/variable "x" t/Int)
      question (q/prove (t/ge (t/add x (t/int 1)) (t/int 1))
                 {:id :increment
                  :profile :math/unbounded-int-v1
                  :assumptions [(t/ge x (t/int 0))]
                  :observe {"x" x}})]
 [(logic/run {:backend :z3} question)
  (logic/run {:backend :oxiz} question)])
#+end_src

The shared layer also implements scalar projection enumeration, bounded integer optimization, finite-grammar synthesis and derived deletion explanations. Passing api/backend assertions cover concrete scenarios; those algorithms do not become native Optimize, MaxSMT, unrestricted synthesis or certificate-producing proof systems.

Advanced value prototypes add bit-field layouts, explicit byte order, pack/unpack lenses and wrapping/checked words. Their floating outcomes use declared binary16 with RNE; all 31 expected advanced results matched on Z3, while OxiZ retained twelve unknowns and two unsupported results. This does not qualify BEAM binary64 source arithmetic. Arithmetic replay limits remain explicit even when the native solver proves an obligation.

A total SMT Array has no finite extent or absence; a finite BL Map needs presence and extent descriptors. A Seq is not an effectful lazy stream. Finite sets, bags, maps and tagged sums are explicit language encodings above primitive terms, so their qualification is separate from native Set/Bag/recursive-datatype APIs. SMT FP equality, IEEE fp-eq and raw bit identity are also different relations: SMT has one NaN value, payload retention needs raw bits, and source-native BEAM floats exclude NaN and infinity.

** Define the boundaries
#+begin_terms
term | immutable typed expression whose meaning is independent of a solver
language value | payload plus runtime-definedness, representation bounds, constraints and decoding domain
native AST | private backend object retained behind an owner and generation
theory | a semantic vocabulary and the laws it denotes
procedure | a verification or search method over terms, such as induction or repair
observation | a captured runtime fact whose freshness and authority remain outside SMT
qualified capability | retained evidence for an operation, sort, result kind, profile and bound
certificate | checkable evidence, separate from a solver verdict or model concreteness
#+end_terms

** Semantic design review
#+begin_honest :title "Preview is an opportunity to choose coherent laws"
Observed behavior is evidence to review. It is not an immutable compatibility requirement. These original canaries retain their original SDK observations. The numeric equality and modulus repairs are now qualified on the selected SDK, as explained above; the other semantic alternatives remain proposals. Historical observations must not be rewritten to match a new runtime.
#+end_honest

#+begin_code_graph :id semantic-design :title "Choose the laws before the interfaces harden" :start vector-access
node vector-access | concept | Strict indexing, defaulting lookup and explicit append | proposed
current Pinned runtime: missing/negative nth returned nil, get used an explicit default, assoc at count appended [10 20], and assoc past count raised ArgumentError. Noninteger-index behavior remains source-inferred.
rule Successful indexed update preserves vector length; strict indexing cannot silently turn a missing element into a nil payload.
field Preserve | Keep permissive nth and append-at-count assoc; encode every branch exactly.
field Change | Make nth strict and assoc replace-only; use get/default for absence and conj for append.
field Split | Expose explicit at/lookup/update/append operations, then choose aliases separately.
proposed Recommendation: split. Separate absence from a programming error and replacement from shape change. Prefer strict nth and replace-only assoc as future default aliases after reviewing uses.
field Consequence | Revisit vector construction/update callers that rely on append-at-count.
field Consequence | Model strict operations with named bounds errors.
field Consequence | Keep representation-capacity :within separate from runtime :defined.
source priv/host/vector.bl:385 | nth | Current-source anchor; concrete runtime receipt is linked separately.
source priv/host/vector.bl:419 | assoc-index | Current-source anchor; concrete runtime receipt is linked separately.
field Affected tests | test/bl/host_vector_test.bl:156; test/bl/host_vector_test.bl:200; test/bl/core_additions_test.bl:21
check Runtime basis | Observed concrete calls on pinned SDK; vector-nth-missing, vector-nth-negative, vector-get-default, vector-assoc-at-count, vector-assoc-past-count. General laws and proposed profiles remain separately unqualified.
field Current profile | bl/current-vector-access; retained runtime observations plus separate current-source evidence.
field Proposed profiles | proposal/vector-access/preserve-v1; proposal/vector-access/change-v1; proposal/vector-access/split-v1; design only, not installed.
source research/formal-logic/evidence/semantic-canaries.json | vector-access | Exact observation IDs above; raw receipt SHA256 in atlas.json.
node missing-nil | concept | Absence is a result, nil is a value | proposed
current Pinned runtime distinguished present nil from absent/default and contains? reported present nil; get-in retained present nil. Historical nil kind labels serialize as null; the return outcome is retained.
rule Storing nil must not erase membership; lookup should support distinguishing missing without inventing a sentinel in user code.
field Preserve | Keep nil-punning get and sentinel/default idioms.
field Change | Make primary lookup return Option(Value), with Some(nil) distinct from None.
field Split | Add lookup/find returning Option and retain get as explicit defaulting convenience.
proposed Recommendation: split. Option lookup gives formal and ordinary code a faithful absence value while a small defaulting convenience remains useful. Decide the public names before the API hardens.
field Consequence | Map lowering needs a presence set plus values, or an array of Option.
field Consequence | Model decoding must preserve None, Some(nil), unavailable and default separately.
field Consequence | Query/pull/JSON-facing convenience APIs need explicit absence policy.

source priv/boot/rt.bl:2120 | coll-get | Current-source anchor; concrete runtime receipt is linked separately.
field Affected tests | test/bl/core_additions_test.bl:29; test/bl/datom/pull_test.bl; test/bl/datom/frame_test.bl
check Runtime basis | Observed concrete calls on pinned SDK; map-present-nil-default, map-absent-default, map-contains-nil, get-in-present-nil. General laws and proposed profiles remain separately unqualified.
field Current profile | bl/current-presence; retained runtime observations plus separate current-source evidence.
field Proposed profiles | proposal/missing-nil/preserve-v1; proposal/missing-nil/change-v1; proposal/missing-nil/split-v1; design only, not installed.
source research/formal-logic/evidence/semantic-canaries.json | missing-nil | Exact observation IDs above; raw receipt SHA256 in atlas.json.
node equality | concept | Value equivalence, numeric equality and representation identity | proposed
current The original canary SDK confirmed a concrete transitivity violation: (= 1.5M 1.5) and (= 1.5 1.50M) are true while (= 1.5M 1.50M) is false. Vector/list content equality is true while native identity is false; conj still observes their kinds.
rule Each equality relation must be reflexive/symmetric/transitive on its declared domain; hash and index identity must respect the chosen relation. Substitutivity is scoped to operations that respect that equivalence.
field Preserve | Preserve existing family equivalence and Decimal exception, but expose its exact behavior.
field Change | Make default equality kind/representation-sensitive; provide numeric= and sequential-equiv? explicitly.
field Split | Use a coherent value-equivalence relation and a separate representation relation; remove the Decimal mixed-policy exception.
proposed Recommendation: split. The concrete transitivity defect was confirmed on the original canary SDK and is repaired for finite numbers on the selected SDK. Remove the scale-sensitive two-Decimal exception from generic value equality, retaining explicit representation/quantization equality. Preserve cross-kind sequence value equality if desired; require the stronger relation for kind-sensitive rewrites. Typed t/eq remains SMT identity.
field Consequence | Audit map/set hashing, sorted indexes, uniqueness/upsert/retract and Datom codecs as one change.
field Consequence | Cross-kind equality does not imply conj/type predicates are interchangeable.
field Consequence | Do not key cache identity by source display strings or hashes alone.

check Runtime basis | Observed concrete calls on pinned SDK; sequence-value-equality, sequence-conj-observability, decimal-float-equality-bridge. General laws and proposed profiles remain separately unqualified.
field Current profile | bl/current-value-equality; retained runtime observations plus separate current-source evidence.

field Qualified numeric repair | Generic finite-number equality now uses exact values; decimal/same-representation? retains coefficient and scale. Ordinary collection key identity remains separate.
rule Numeric semantic keys already reduce exact rationals. A changed equality can collapse pre-existing container entries; define collision/value precedence and validate retained indexes and lossless payloads.
source research/formal-logic/repro/equality-transitivity.bl | formal.equality-coherence/witness | Permanent explicit known-failing observation; assert-coherent! can become a focused runtime regression after a semantic fix.
node decimal-scale | concept | A decimal number and a quantized amount can be different types | proposed
current Original canary SDK: 1.5M and 1.50M have different coefficients/scales, generic equality was false, and numeric == is true. Exact-division refusal and rounding rules remain source-backed beyond these canaries.
rule Numeric equality should be coherent, and scale-sensitive financial intent should remain expressible and explicit.
field Preserve | One scale-aware Decimal type; all equality/coercion rules must then consistently honor representation.
field Change | Normalize Decimal to a numeric value and make scale presentation metadata.
field Split | Separate normalized Decimal value from Quantized(amount, scale, rounding-policy) representation.
proposed Recommendation: split. Separate mathematical value from quantization intent so money contracts can retain cents without making generic equality nontransitive.
field Consequence | Define literal M meaning and migrations before changing normalization.
field Consequence | Keep exact division and rounded division visibly distinct.
field Consequence | Check hash/index canonicalization and lossless payload codecs independently.

node real-float | concept | Mathematical specifications and finite machine floats are separate domains | proposed
current Pinned runtime: (/ 7 2) returned binary64 3.5; (+ 0.1 0.2) returned 0.30000000000000004; overflow and float division by zero raised ArithmeticError; Decimal/Float arithmetic raised; signed zeros had distinct encodings but generic equality true.
rule A proof about machine arithmetic must preserve rounding, finite-result restrictions and raisedness; a mathematical Real specification must be labelled as mathematical.
field Preserve | Retain Real abstraction, explicitly label it abstract and require a refinement argument for machine claims.
field Change | Use finite binary64 semantics by default for native float functions.
field Split | Expose Real, raw IEEE and BL finite-float Outcome profiles separately.
proposed Recommendation: split. Default source-native verification to the runtime profile; use Real only when requested for mathematical specification or a justified abstraction.
field Consequence | FP sort/rounding constraints and error outcomes must flow through ANF lowering.
field Consequence | Cache identity includes the numeric profile.
field Consequence | Mixed Int/Float/Decimal coercion needs a reviewed, testable contract.

node division-sign | concept | Name truncating, flooring and Euclidean division explicitly | proposed
current Pinned runtime: quot/rem(-7,3) returned [-2,-1], mod(7,-3)/mod(-7,-3) returned [-2,-1], and integer zero division raised. The general sign laws remain source-backed and require broader law qualification.
rule Every quotient/remainder pair must satisfy a=b*q+r with its documented sign/range rule; names must identify the selected rule.
field Preserve | Keep names and encode sign corrections in each backend-independent profile.
field Change | Choose one division family as the only generic integer division surface.

proposed Recommendation: split. All three conventions have legitimate uses. Explicit families prevent a solver primitive from silently changing a BL operation.
field Consequence | Audit the existing direct mod mapping for negative divisors.
field Consequence | Keep division-by-zero as an outcome.
field Consequence | Use a full four-sign canary matrix and algebraic laws, not only positive examples.

node text-indices | concept | Bytes, codepoints and graphemes deserve named indices | proposed
current Pinned runtime: decomposed e+acute counted as 1 grapheme, 2 codepoints and 3 UTF-8 bytes. Separately counted e and combining acute had [1,1] graphemes while their concatenation counted 1.
rule An index and length carry the same text unit as the operation consuming them. Conversion is explicit and depends on a declared Unicode profile.
field Preserve | Keep grapheme defaults and refuse SMT lowering unless equivalence is established.
field Change | Use codepoints as a simpler core sequence, with grapheme UI functions explicit.
field Split | Expose bytes/codepoints/graphemes views with distinct indexed types and conversion operations.
proposed Recommendation: split. Use codepoint strings for directly supported symbolic theory and retain a named grapheme view for user-facing text; choose ergonomic aliases after reviewing editing APIs.
field Consequence | Do not lower BL count to str.len by name alone.
field Consequence | Pin Unicode segmentation version or supply finite segmentation evidence.
field Consequence | Grapheme count is not generally additive across concatenation.

node truthiness | concept | Boolean logic and conditional coercion are separate operations | proposed
current Pinned runtime: nil and false were falsey; zero, empty text and empty vector were truthy; (or nil 0 :unreached) returned integer 0.
rule Logical connectives on Bool obey Boolean laws; conditional coercion must not silently identify an arbitrary value with true or false.
field Preserve | Keep broad truthiness in all source conditionals.
field Change | Require Bool in conditions; add explicit boolean coercion.
field Split | Keep ordinary truthiness as a language feature, but require Bool terms in formal logic and lower truthiness explicitly in source verification.
proposed Recommendation: split. Bool-only term construction prevents symbolic coercion bugs without forcing a language-wide conditional change before reviewing usage.
field Consequence | ANF already records branch structure and operand-return semantics; preserve both.
field Consequence | Review convenience guards that use non-Boolean values intentionally.
field Consequence | Unknown Any-valued predicates need a truthiness term or explicit unsupported result.

current Pinned runtime: vector/list native identity was false despite content equality, and metadata did not affect value equality. Live ownership/generation laws remain runtime-source evidence, not established by these data canaries.
rule Portable term identity is content/semantics based; native handle identity is owner/generation based; neither grants runtime authority.
field Preserve | Continue implicit identity conventions at each boundary.
field Change | Make all equality representation-sensitive, including storage and metadata.
field Split | Declare semantic value keys, lossless representation keys and runtime resource references as distinct types.
proposed Recommendation: split. This permits portable DAG caching while preserving lossless payloads and strict native lifetime checks. Opaque live resources remain observations.
field Consequence | Native DAG IDs are never placed inside portable terms.
field Consequence | Cache keys include profile, bounds, backend artifact and query mode.
field Consequence | A stale-generation handle is refused even when its integer ID is reused.

edge vector-access -> missing-nil | indexed absence needs an explicit outcome
edge missing-nil -> equality | map membership and key identity must remain coherent
edge equality -> decimal-scale | scale-sensitive identity causes the confirmed numeric bridge defect
edge equality -> data-identity | value equivalence differs from representation and authority
edge decimal-scale -> real-float | explicit conversions separate exact and machine arithmetic
edge real-float -> division-sign | runtime errors and rounding change arithmetic laws
edge text-indices -> data-identity | indexed views change representation without granting authority
edge truthiness -> missing-nil | nil in conditionals is distinct from absent lookup
edge truthiness -> equality | Boolean logic and source value coercion need different lowering
#+end_code_graph

** Evidence that can travel
#+begin_rosetta
Question | Evidence to retain | What it establishes
Is the formula satisfiable? | raw backend status with artifact, problem digest and settings | the backend verdict for that exact question
Can a model be displayed? | available, partial, opaque or unavailable observations | presentation and decoding availability
Does the witness satisfy the language claim? | independent evaluation under the declared profile | validation of that candidate witness
Is an UNSAT result independently checked? | certificate plus checker identity and result | the checked certificate claim
Do both engines agree? | separate result records for the same typed problem | an agreement observation, not a proof rule
Can this become a compiler fact? | authority policy, source generation, semantic profile and cache identity | admissibility for that consumer only
#+end_rosetta

** Executable evidence workflow
#+begin_sequence :title "From declaration to a comparable observation"
lanes author · BL source | model · typed terms | owner · native session | evidence · receipt
author -> model : select semantic profile, source snapshot and explicit bounds
model -> model : sort-check, collect constraints and runtime outcomes
model -> owner : send the same DAG, assertions and requested observations
owner -> owner : select Z3 or OxiZ; retain native identities by generation
owner --> evidence : raw status, observations, availability, cores and cost
evidence -> model : independently validate concrete witnesses where supported
evidence => author : show scope, validation and source provenance together
note author, evidence : unsupported and unknown remain visible; no backend vote changes authority
#+end_sequence

** Review paths
- Read atlas.json for machine-readable families, source hashes and semantic alternatives.
- Run source-index.bl under the pinned project runner to discover definitions and qualified calls through Codebase.
- Run semantic-canaries.bl under the same runner to observe the actual SDK behavior.
- Use retained query receipts to attach implemented and qualified status; do not infer either from this catalogue.
- Review connected cards at phone and desktop widths with the codebase-review host. A browser inspection is required before claiming the review surface is qualified.
